d5 is (4,5). g4 is (7,4). |4-7|=3, |5-4|=1. Not same diagonal.

What about c6c5?
c5: (3,5). 3+5=8 (Black).
Diagonal check for Bishop g4 (white): Cannot attack.

So all three moves are safe from the Bishop.
What about the Rooks?
Rook b1: File b.
Rook h8: Rank 8.
Neither attacks c7, d5, or c5.

The Knight on c8:
Attacks a7, b6, d6, e7.
Neither c7, d5, nor c5 are attacked by the knight.

So c6c7, c6d5, c6c5 are all legal and safe from immediate capture.
c6c7 puts pressure on the knight. In a desperate situation, this is often the best way to play - force the opponent to respond.
